You: what about this: how do i make a new drive
Bot: Run drive new NAME for a new folder as a drive, or drive new NAME PATH for a folder you have. A size like 2G is allowed.
You: ok so what is your name
Bot: I am xaiorin, xr for short, a small model that runs on your phone.
You: what about this: how do i change the theme
Bot: Type jaric theme NAME, for example jaric theme amber.
You: hey, uninstall a tool
Bot: Run jc unstall NAME. jsh and jget cannot be removed.
You: ok so what is jaric
Bot: Jaric is a terminal app with its own shell, tools and a language.
You: i want to know how do i see a video file
Bot: Run jaric video FILE.
You: quick question: show files
Bot: Run dir. Use dir -a to see hidden ones.
You: help me: what is j2k
Bot: J2K is the language of Jaric. Its compiler is jcmp.
You: kill a program please
Bot: Run jamanager kill PID.
You: help me: install python
Bot: Run jc install python. Then python3 and pip work.

Bot: Type mujic. Then write part of a song name and press Enter.

Bot: Run cat FILE. Use head or tail for a part.

Bot: After jcmp hello.jk -o hello type ./hello.

Bot: Run unj FILE.zip.

Bot: Run jamanager mem.

Bot: Run cat FILE. Use head or tail for a part.

Bot: Run jamanager kill PID.

Bot: Run cp FROM TO. Use cp -r for a folder.
You: help me: hi
Bot: Hi! I am xaiorin, your helper. Ask me about the tools.
You: quick question: how much memory is free
Bot: Run jamanager mem.
You: how do i restart the app now
Bot: Type jaric restart.
You: hey, how do i make a new drive
Bot: Run drive new NAME for a new folder as a drive, or drive new NAME PATH for a folder you have. A size like 2G is allowed.
You: please update jcmp
Bot: Run jc install jcmp. makej needs jcmp 1.2.0.0 or newer.
You: help me: how do i see a video file
Bot: Run jaric video FILE.
You: can you tell me import songs
Bot: In mujic type /import PATH, or put the files in the folder music.
You: hey, install yt-dlp
Bot: Run jc install python yt-dlp. Use it only for what you may download.
You: what about this: new session
Bot: Tap + at the top, or type start PROGRAM.
You: what about this: what is f: and 0:
Bot: f:/ is your home, 0:/ is the storage of the phone, r:/ is the root. drive shows them.

Bot: jget -jsh URL. Only use addresses you trust.
You: ok so remove a file
Bot: Run rm FILE.

Bot: In mujic type /import PATH, or put the files in the folder music.

Bot: Run mv FROM TO. It also renames.
You: new project?
Bot: Make a folder and run makej init -pro NAME.
You: ok so how do i see the real path of a drive
Bot: Run drive -p :/bin. drive -d PATH goes the other way.
You: how do i use git
Bot: Run jgit. It works on local repositories: init, add, commit, status, log, diff.
You: tell me what is jc
Bot: jc installs and runs the programs of Jaric: jc install NAME, jc update, jc unstall NAME, jc list.
You: can you tell me how do i sort songs into folders
Bot: In mujic type /mv NAME FOLDER.

Bot: Run pwd. pwd -p shows the real path.
